0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">MCP-Discord provides the following Discord-related functionalities:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Login to Discord bot</li>\n<li>List servers the bot is a member of</li>\n<li>Get server information</li>\n<li>List members and inspect member details</li>\n<li>List, create, edit, delete, assign, and remove roles</li>\n<li>Search messages in a server</li>\n<li>Read, edit, and delete channel messages</li>\n<li>Send messages to specified channels</li>\n<li>Retrieve forum channel lists</li>\n<li>Create, update, delete, and reply to forum posts</li>\n<li>List forum threads and manage forum tags</li>\n<li>Create, edit, and delete text, forum, voice channels, and categories</li>\n<li>Set and remove channel permission overrides</li>\n<li>Add/remove message reactions</li>\n<li>Create/edit/delete/use webhooks</li>\n</ul>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"pl-c\"><span class=\"pl-c\">#</span> Override the listening port if needed</span>\ndocker run -e DISCORD_TOKEN=your_discord_bot_token -p 3000:3000 barryy625/mcp-discord:latest --transport http
